\"oncocooler_analysis.json\"\nSUMMARY_PATH = ROOT / \"oncocooler_summary.md\"\n\nDAYS_PER_DISPATCH_10_DAY = 10\nDISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_10_DAY = 36\nDAYS_PER_DISPATCH_20_DAY = 20\nDISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_20_DAY = 18\nDECISION_THRESHOLD_USD = Decimal(\"10000\")\n\n\ndef money(value):\n    \"\"\"Round a Decimal currency value to two places and return a JSON number.\"\"\"\n    return float(Decimal(value).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP))\n\n\ndef dec(value):\n    return Decimal(str(value))\n\n\ndef read_csv_rows(path):\n    with path.open(newline=\"\") as handle:\n        return list(csv.DictReader(handle))\n\n\ndef load_in_scope_programs():\n    with CATALOG_PATH.open() as handle:\n        catalog = json.load(handle)\n\n    programs = []\n    for programs.append(program)\n    return programs\n\n\ndef load_payments(programs):\n    label_to_code = {}\n    for program in programs:\n        labels = [program[\"program_name\"], *program.get(\"known_labels\", [])]\n        for label in labels:\n            label_to_code[label] = program[\"program_code\"]\n\n    payments = {}\n    for row in read_csv_rows(CONTRACT_PAYMENT_PATH):\n        program_code = label_to_code.get(row[\"program_label\"])\n        if program_code is not None:\n            payments[program_code] = best_approved_overrides.get(program_code)\n        if current is None or version_no > current[\"version_no\"]:\n            best_approved_overrides[program_code] = {\n                \"version_no\": version_no,\n                \"active_sites\": int(row[\"active_sites\"]),\n            }\n\n    active_sites = {}\n    for program in programs:\n        program_code = program[\"program_code\"]\n        override = best_approved_overrides.get(program_code)\n        if override is None:\n            active_sites[program_code] = int(program[\"default_active_sites\"])\n        else:\n            active_sites[program_code] = override[\"active_sites\"]\n    return active_sites\n\n\ndef load_cooler_costs():\n    return {\n        row[\"cooler_type\"]: dec(row[\"cooler_cost_usd\"])\n        for acquisition_cost * active_sites * units_per_day * days_per_dispatch * dispatches_per_year / Decimal(\"1000\")\n    cooler_total = cooler_cost * active_sites * dispatches_per_year\n    revenue = payment * active_sites * dispatches_per_year\n    margin = revenue - drug_cost - cooler_total\n    return drug_cost, cooler_total, revenue, margin\n\n\ndef main():\n    programs = load_in_scope_programs()\n    payments = load_payments(programs)\n    active_sites_by_code = load_active_sites(programs)\n    cooler_costs = load_cooler_costs()\n\n    output_programs = []\n    total_margin_10 = Decimal(\"0\")\n    total_margin_20 = Decimal(\"0\")\n    total_difference = Decimal(\"0\")\n\n    for program in sorted(programs, key=lambda item: item[\"program_code\"]):\n          Decimal(DISPATCHES_PER_YEAR_20_DAY),\n        )\n        difference = margin_20 - margin_10\n\n        total_margin_10 += margin_10\n        total_margin_20 += margin_20\n        total_difference += difference\n\n        output_programs.append(\n            {\n                \"program_code\": program_code,\n                \"program_name\": program[\"program_name\"],\n                \"active_sites\": int(active_sites),\n                \"cooler_type\": program[\"cooler_type\"],\n                \"cooler_cost_usd\": money(cooler_cost),\n \"annual_margin_10_day\": money(margin_10),\n                \"annual_drug_cost_20_day\": money(drug_20),\n                \"annual_cooler_cost_20_day\": money(cooler_20),\n                \"annual_revenue_20_day\": money(revenue_20),\n                \"annual_margin_20_day\": money(margin_20),\n                \"annual_margin_difference_20_minus_10\": money(difference),\n            }\n        )\n\n    total_margin_10_usd = money(total_margin_10)\n    total_margin_20_usd = money(total_margin_20)\n    total_difference_usd = money(total_difference)\n    absolute_difference_usd = money(abs(total_difference))\n\n    decision = \"move_to_20_day\" if abs(total_difference) < DECISION_THRESHOLD_USD else \"keep_10_day\"\n    justification = (\n        f\"The absolute total annual margin difference is ${absolute_difference_usd:,.2f}, \"\n        f\"which is {'below' if decision == 'move_to_20_day' else 'not below'} \"\n        f\"the ${DECISION_THRESHOLD_USD:,.2f} threshold.\"\n    )\n\n    output = {\n        \"assumptions\": {\n            \"days_per_dispatch_10_day\": DAYS_PER_DISPATCH_10_DAY,\n            \"dispatches_per_year_10_day\":       \"total_annual_margin_difference_20_minus_10_usd\": total_difference_usd,\n            \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": absolute_difference_usd,\n        },\n        \"recommendation\": {\n            \"decision\": decision,\n            \"justification\": justification,\n        },\n    }\n\n    with ANALYSIS_PATH.open(\"w\") as handle:\n        json.dump(output, handle, indent=2)\n
